GEA_pacify_indochina_decisions = {#平定印度支那
	GEA_nationalist_sabotage_cycle = { #民族主义者正在策划破坏
		icon = GFX_decision_generic_military
		allowed = { always = no }
		activation = { always = no }
		available = { always = no }
		selectable_mission = no
		days_mission_timeout = 30
		is_good = no

		visible = {
			has_global_flag = GEA_Indochina_Uprising_Fired
		}
		cancel_trigger = {
			has_global_flag = GEA_Indochina_Uprising_Fired
			NOT = { country_exists = INC }
		}

		timeout_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision timeout GEA_nationalist_sabotage_cycle"
			GEA_roll_nationalist_sabotage_event_effect = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_global_flag = GEA_Indochina_Uprising_Fired
					country_exists = INC
				}
				activate_mission = GEA_nationalist_sabotage_cycle
			}
		}
	}
	GEA_designate_priority_defense_zone = { #指定重点防御区
		icon = GFX_decision_bunker
		allowed = { original_tag = GEA }

		custom_cost_text = CP_10
		custom_cost_trigger = { command_power > 9.99 }

		visible = {
			has_completed_focus = GEA_Redraw_the_defense_zone
			check_variable = { GEA_priority_defense_zones_selected < 5 }
		}

		target_trigger = {
			FROM = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = GEA
				NOT = { has_state_flag = GEA_priority_defense_zone }
			}
		}

		state_target = yes
		on_map_mode = map_only

		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 80

			modifier = {
				factor = 0.25
				NOT = { has_political_power > 50 }
			}
		}

		complete_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision GEA_designate_priority_defense_zone"
			add_command_power = -10
			FROM = {
				set_state_flag = GEA_priority_defense_zone
				add_state_modifier = {
					modifier = {
						local_manpower = 0.15
						local_org_regain = 0.1
						local_supplies = 0.1
						state_production_speed_bunker_factor = 0.10
					}
				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = GEA_designate_priority_defense_zone_effect_tt
			}
			add_to_variable = { GEA_priority_defense_zones_selected = 1 }
		}
	}
	GEA_register_loyal_civic_associations = { #登记市民团体

		}
	}

					days = 365
					track = infantry
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { GEA_review_infantry_combat_lessons_count = 1 }
				}
				add_tech_bonus = {
					bonus = 0.5
					uses = 1
					category = infantry_weapons
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_mastery_bonus = {
					name = GEA_review_infantry_combat_lessons_2
					bonus = 0.1
					days = 365
					track = operations
				}
			}
		}

					industrial_complex > 0

				}

				factor = 0.25

			}
		}

		complete_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision GEA_relocate_indochina_industry target: [From.GetName]"
			custom_effect_tooltip = GEA_relocate_indochina_industry_start_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				FROM = {
					if = {
						limit = { arms_factory > 0 }
						set_state_flag = GEA_relocated_arms_factory
						remove_building = {
							type = arms_factory
							level = 1
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = { industrial_complex > 0 }
						set_state_flag = GEA_relocated_industrial_complex
						remove_building = {
							type = industrial_complex
							level = 1
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

		remove_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision remove GEA_relocate_indochina_industry target: [From.GetName]"
			custom_effect_tooltip = GEA_relocate_indochina_industry_complete_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = { FROM = { has_state_flag = GEA_relocated_arms_factory } }
					random_owned_controlled_state = {
						limit = {
							NOT = {
								OR = {
									state = 857
									state = 671
									state = 827
									state = 286
									state = 856
									state = 670
									state = 826
								}
							}
						}
						add_building_construction = {
							type = arms_factory
							level = 1
							instant_build = yes
						}
					}
					FROM = { clr_state_flag = GEA_relocated_arms_factory }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { FROM = { has_state_flag = GEA_relocated_industrial_complex } }
					random_owned_controlled_state = {
						limit = {
							NOT = {
								OR = {
									state = 857
									state = 671
									state = 827
									state = 286
									state = 856
									state = 670
									state = 826
								}
							}
						}
						add_building_construction = {
							type = industrial_complex
							level = 1
							instant_build = yes
						}
					}
					FROM = { clr_state_flag = GEA_relocated_industrial_complex }
				}
			}
		}
	}

		allowed = { original_tag = GEA }

		custom_cost_text = CP_50
		custom_cost_trigger = { command_power > 49.99 }
		days_remove = 120
		days_re_enable = 120

		visible = {
			has_country_flag = GEA_east_china_investment_unlocked
			OR = { has_country_flag = LEP_OPEN is_ai = yes }
			FROM = { GEA_china_warlord_can_interact_trigger = yes }
		}
		targets_dynamic = yes
		target_trigger = { FROM = { owns_state = 613 } }
		available = {
			NOT = { has_war_with = FROM }
			FROM = {
				owns_state = 613
				controls_state = 613
			}
			has_manpower > 1999
		}
		cancel_trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_war_with = FROM
				FROM = {
					OR = {
						NOT = { owns_state = 613 }
						NOT = { controls_state = 613 }
						GEA_china_warlord_cannot_interact_trigger = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = { factor = 20 
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.25
				NOT = { has_political_power > 50 }
			}
		
			# FROM is the actual recipient, including a new tag that owns the target region.
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				FROM = { is_ai = no }
			}
		}
		
		modifier = { experience_gain_army_factor = -0.05 }
		complete_effect = {
			add_command_power = -50
			add_manpower = -2000
		}
		remove_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision remove GEA_Dispatch_a_Training_Division target: [From.GetName]"
			FROM = {
				army_experience = 20
				add_doctrine_cost_reduction = {
					name = GEA_Dispatch_a_Training_Division
					cost_reduction = 0.25
					uses = 1
					category = land_doctrine
				}
				add_mastery_bonus = {
					name = GEA_Dispatch_a_Training_Division
					bonus = 0.05
					days = 120
					folder = land
				}
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = GEA_china_lep_name_tt
			add_to_variable = {
				GEA_china_lep_military_projection = 15
				tooltip = GEA_china_military_projection_change_tt
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				clamp_variable = { var = GEA_china_lep_military_projection min = 0 max = 100 }
			}
		}
	}
	GEA_Expand_military_equipment_exports = { #扩大军械出口
		icon = GFX_GEA_Invest_in_East_China_decision
		allowed = { original_tag = GEA }


		cost = 0
		custom_cost_text = GEA_lep_export_cp_cost
		custom_cost_trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					has_completed_focus = GEA_The_usiness_expansion_of_AOG
					command_power > 19.99
				}
				AND = {
					NOT = { has_completed_focus = GEA_The_usiness_expansion_of_AOG }
					command_power > 29.99
				}
			}
		}
		days_remove = 60
		days_re_enable = GEA_lep_export_cooldown?120

		visible = {
			FROM = { GEA_china_warlord_can_interact_trigger = yes }
			has_completed_focus = GEA_Goods_out_money_in
			has_country_flag = GEA_east_china_investment_unlocked
			OR = { has_country_flag = LEP_OPEN is_ai = yes }
		}

		targets_dynamic = yes
		target_trigger = {
			FROM = { owns_state = 613 }
		}
		available = {
			FROM = { owns_state = 613 }
		}

		modifier = {
			political_power_gain = 0.03
			industrial_capacity_factory = -0.10
			consumer_goods_factor = -0.05
		}

		complete_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision GEA_Expand_military_equipment_exports target: [From.GetName]"
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = GEA_The_usiness_expansion_of_AOG }
					add_command_power = -20
				}
				else = { add_command_power = -30 }
			}
			FROM = { add_ideas = GEA_German_military_equipment_imports }
		}
		remove_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision remove GEA_Expand_military_equipment_exports target: [From.GetName]"
			FROM = {
				remove_ideas = GEA_German_military_equipment_imports
				add_popularity = { ideology = market_liberal popularity = 0.02 }
				GEA_refresh_tsingtau_collaboration_effect = yes
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = GEA_china_lep_name_tt
			custom_effect_tooltip = GEA_china_attitude_add_5_tt
			custom_effect_tooltip = GEA_china_capital_influence_add_5_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				add_to_variable = { GEA_china_lep_attitude = 5 }
				clamp_variable = { var = GEA_china_lep_attitude min = 0 max = 100 }
				add_to_variable = { GEA_china_lep_capital_influence = 5 }
				clamp_variable = { var = GEA_china_lep_capital_influence min = 0 max = 100 }
			}
		}
		cancel_effect = {
			FROM = { remove_ideas = GEA_German_military_equipment_imports }
		}
		cancel_trigger = {
			OR = {
				FROM = {
					exists = no
				}
				FROM = {
					GEA_china_warlord_cannot_interact_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 20
		
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.25
				NOT = { has_political_power > 50 }
			}
		
			# FROM is the actual recipient, including a new tag that owns the target region.
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				FROM = { is_ai = no }
			}
		}
	}

		}

			}

				NOT = { has_political_power > 90 }
			}

			# FROM is the actual recipient, including a new tag that owns the target region.
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				FROM = { is_ai = no }
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {

							is_controlled_by = PREV
							infrastructure < 5
						}

					}
				}

				}

					tooltip = GEA_china_attitude_change_tt

							level = 1

				GEA_china_szc_capital_influence = -20
				tooltip = GEA_china_capital_influence_change_tt
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				GEA_china_szc_military_projection = -15
				tooltip = GEA_china_military_projection_change_tt
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				GEA_china_szc_attitude = -15
				tooltip = GEA_china_attitude_change_tt
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				add_command_power = -30
				clamp_variable = {
					var = GEA_china_szc_capital_influence
					min = 0
					max = 100
				}
				clamp_variable = {
					var = GEA_china_szc_military_projection
					min = 0
					max = 100
				}
				clamp_variable = {
					var = GEA_china_szc_attitude
					min = 0
					max = 100
				}
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					GEA_sichuan_non_anti_concession_controller_trigger = yes
					NOT = {
						has_war_with = SZC
					}
					SZC = {
						custom_trigger_tooltip = {
							tooltip = GEA_szc_two_military_factories_available_tt
							OR = {
								any_owned_state = {
									is_controlled_by = SZC
									arms_factory > 1
								}
								any_owned_state = {
									is_controlled_by = SZC
									arms_factory > 0
									SZC = {
										any_owned_state = {
											is_controlled_by = SZC
											arms_factory > 0
											NOT = {
												state = PREV.PREV
											}
										}
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision remove GEA_szc_relocate_armament_lines"
				SZC = {
					random_owned_controlled_state = {
						limit = {
							arms_factory > 0
						}
						remove_building = {
							type = arms_factory
							level = 1
						}
					}
					random_owned_controlled_state = {
						limit = {
							arms_factory > 0
						}
						remove_building = {
							type = arms_factory
							level = 1
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						any_owned_state = {
							is_controlled_by = ROOT
							free_building_slots = {
								building = arms_factory
								size > 1
								include_locked = yes
							}
						}
					}
					random_owned_controlled_state = {
						limit = {
							free_building_slots = {
								building = arms_factory
								size > 1
								include_locked = yes
							}
						}
						add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 2
						add_building_construction = {
							type = arms_factory
							level = 2
							instant_build = yes
						}
					}
				}
				else = {
					add_offsite_building = {
						type = arms_factory
						level = 2
					}
				}
				army_experience = 25
			}
		}
	}

					}
				}

		allowed = { original_tag = GEA }
		priority = 10001

		cost = 0
		days_remove = 30
		fire_only_once = yes
		cancel_if_not_visible = no

		visible = {
			has_completed_focus = GEA_Protect_our_property_rights
			OR = { has_country_flag = QIE_OPEN is_ai = yes }
			QIE = { exists = yes }
		}
		targets = { QIE }

		available = {
			NOT = { has_war_with = QIE }
			NOT = { is_in_faction_with = QIE }
			QIE = {  NOT = { is_subject_of = GEA } }
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = GEA_north_china_expedition_support_tt
				OR = {
					SQI = {
						exists = yes
						OR = { is_in_faction_with = GEA is_subject_of = GEA }
					}
					613 = {
						owner = {
							NOT = { tag = GEA }
							OR = { is_in_faction_with = GEA is_subject_of = GEA }
						}
					}
					LEC = {
						exists = yes
						OR = { is_in_faction_with = GEA is_subject_of = GEA }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		cancel_trigger = {
			OR = {
				QIE = { exists = no }
				has_war_with = QIE
				is_in_faction_with = QIE
				QIE = { is_subject_of = GEA }
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = { factor = 0
			# FROM is the actual recipient, including a new tag that owns the target region.
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				FROM = { is_ai = no }
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision remove GEA_North_China_Expedition"
			declare_war_on = { target = QIE type = annex_everything }
		}
	}

	GEA_qie_develop_tangshan = { #唐山煤钢联合开发
		icon = GFX_GEA_Invest_in_East_China_decision
		allowed = { original_tag = GEA }

		cost = 0
		custom_cost_text = GEA_qie_develop_tangshan_cost
		custom_cost_trigger = { has_political_power > 59.99 command_power > 39.99 }

		days_remove = 365
		fire_only_once = yes
		cancel_if_not_visible = no

		visible = {
			has_completed_focus = GEA_Establish_a_development_special_zone
			OR = { has_country_flag = QIE_OPEN is_ai = yes }
			QIE = {
				exists = yes GEA_china_warlord_can_interact_trigger = yes
			}
		}

		targets = { QIE }

		available = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = GEA_qie_no_development_in_progress_tt
				NOT = { has_country_flag = GEA_qie_development_in_progress }
			}
			num_of_civilian_factories_available_for_projects > 2
			NOT = { has_war_with = QIE }
			QIE = {

				owns_state = 614
				controls_state = 614
			}
		}

		cancel_trigger = {
			OR = {
				QIE = { exists = no }
				has_war_with = QIE
				QIE = {
					OR = {
						GEA_china_warlord_cannot_interact_trigger = yes
						NOT = { owns_state = 614 }
						NOT = { controls_state = 614 }
					}
				}
			}
		}

		modifier = { civilian_factory_use = 3 }
		ai_will_do = { factor = 120
			# Do not use automated interactions against player-controlled targets.
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = {
					QIE = { exists = yes is_ai = no }
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.25
				NOT = { has_political_power > 50 }
			}

			# FROM is the actual recipient, including a new tag that owns the target region.
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				FROM = { is_ai = no }
			}
		}

		complete_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision GEA_qie_develop_tangshan"
			hidden_effect = {
				add_political_power = -60
				add_command_power = -40
				set_country_flag = GEA_qie_development_in_progress
			}
		}

		cancel_effect = {
			hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = GEA_qie_development_in_progress }
		}

		remove_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision remove GEA_qie_develop_tangshan"
			hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = GEA_qie_development_in_progress }
			614 = {
				add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 5
				add_building_construction = { type = industrial_complex level = 3 instant_build = yes }
				add_building_construction = { type = arms_factory level = 2 instant_build = yes }
				add_building_construction = { type = infrastructure level = 2 instant_build = yes }
			}
			add_resource = { type = steel amount = 16 state = 614 }
			add_resource = { type = coal amount = 4 state = 614 }
			QIE = { add_popularity = { ideology = market_liberal popularity = 0.05 } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = GEA_china_zhili_name_tt
			add_to_variable = {
				GEA_china_zhili_attitude = 5
				tooltip = GEA_china_attitude_change_tt
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				GEA_china_zhili_capital_influence = 15
				tooltip = GEA_china_capital_influence_change_tt
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				GEA_china_zhili_military_projection = 5
				tooltip = GEA_china_military_projection_change_tt
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				clamp_variable = { var = GEA_china_zhili_attitude min = 0 max = 100 }
				clamp_variable = { var = GEA_china_zhili_capital_influence min = 0 max = 100 }
				clamp_variable = { var = GEA_china_zhili_military_projection min = 0 max = 100 }
			}
		}
	}

						type = infrastructure
						level = 1
						instant_build = yes

				}
			}
		}

			has_completed_focus = GEA_The_imperial_pirates_roaming_the_vast_ocean
			#has_country_flag = GEA_pirate_war_target_selected
			has_event_target = GEA_pirate_war_target
			if = {
				limit = { has_event_target = GEA_pirate_war_target }
				event_target:GEA_pirate_war_target = { exists = yes }
			}
		}
		available = {
			has_manpower > 200
			has_navy_size = { size > 50 }

			if = {
				limit = { has_event_target = GEA_pirate_war_target }
				event_target:GEA_pirate_war_target = { has_war_with = ROOT }
			}
			has_country_flag = GEA_pirate_war_target_selected
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = GEA_pirate_raid_capacity_available_tt
				check_variable = { GEA_active_raids < GEA_max_concurrent_raids }
			}
		}
		complete_effect = {
			add_command_power = -50
			add_manpower = -200
			hidden_effect = {
				add_to_variable = { GEA_active_raids = 1 }
				# 行动目标在启动时锁定，不随之后重新选择破交对象而改变
				event_target:GEA_pirate_war_target = { save_global_event_target_as = GEA_naval_port_raid_target }
			}
		}
		cancel_trigger = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_event_target = GEA_naval_port_raid_target }
				NOT = { event_target:GEA_naval_port_raid_target = { exists = yes has_war_with = ROOT } }
			}
			else = { always = yes }
		}
		cancel_effect = {
			hidden_effect = {
				add_to_variable = { GEA_active_raids = -1 }
				clamp_variable = { var = GEA_active_raids min = 0 }
				clear_global_event_target = GEA_naval_port_raid_target
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision remove GEA_Sabotage_enemy_naval_ports"
			if = {
				limit = { has_event_target = GEA_naval_port_raid_target }
				event_target:GEA_naval_port_raid_target = {
					add_timed_idea = { idea = GEA_naval_ports_attacked days = 100 }
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				add_to_variable = { GEA_active_raids = -1 }
				clamp_variable = { var = GEA_active_raids min = 0 }
				clear_global_event_target = GEA_naval_port_raid_target
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 0.1

			# Do not use automated interactions against player-controlled targets.
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = {
					GER = { exists = yes is_ai = no }
					AND = { has_event_target = GEA_pirate_war_target event_target:GEA_pirate_war_target = { exists = yes is_ai = no } }
					AND = { has_event_target = GEA_naval_port_raid_target event_target:GEA_naval_port_raid_target = { exists = yes is_ai = no } }
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.25
				NOT = { has_political_power > 50 }
			}
		}
	}

	GEA_Expand_the_pirate_fleet = { #扩大海盗船队
	 	allowed = { original_tag = GEA }
		icon = GFX_GEA_Expand_the_pirate_fleet

		custom_cost_text = GEA_pirate_fleet_expansion_cost
		custom_cost_trigger = {
			command_power > 49.99
			has_navy_experience > 49.99
		}

		days_re_enable = 60
		days_remove = 60

		visible = {
			check_variable = { GEA_pirate_fleet_expansions < 5 }
		}

		available = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = GEA_pirate_fleet_expansion_threshold_tt
				OR = {
					AND = { # 第一次扩编：50艘
						check_variable = { GEA_pirate_fleet_expansions = 0 }
						has_navy_size = { size > 49 }
					}
					AND = { # 第二次扩编：100艘
						check_variable = { GEA_pirate_fleet_expansions = 1 }
						has_navy_size = { size > 99 }
					}
					AND = { # 第三次扩编：200艘
						check_variable = { GEA_pirate_fleet_expansions = 2 }
						has_navy_size = { size > 199 }
					}
					AND = { # 第四次扩编：300艘
						check_variable = { GEA_pirate_fleet_expansions = 3 }
						has_navy_size = { size > 299 }
					}
					AND = { # 第五次扩编：400艘
						check_variable = { GEA_pirate_fleet_expansions = 4 }
						has_navy_size = { size > 399 }
					}
				}
			}
		}

		complete_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision GEA_Expand_the_pirate_fleet"
			add_command_power = -50
			navy_experience = -50
			add_to_variable = { GEA_pirate_fleet_expansions = 1 } # 启动时消耗当前档次
		}

		remove_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision remove GEA_Expand_the_pirate_fleet"
			custom_override_tooltip = {
				tooltip = GEA_pirate_fleet_expansion_effect_tt
				add_to_variable = { GEA_max_concurrent_raids = 1 }
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 0.1
		
			# Do not use automated interactions against player-controlled targets.
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = {
					GER = { exists = yes is_ai = no }
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.25
				NOT = { has_political_power > 50 }
			}
		}
	}
	GEA_Raid_on_the_Pacific_shipping_routes = { #劫掠太平洋航线
		allowed = { original_tag = GEA }
		icon = GFX_decision_GEA_Raid_on_the_Pacific_shipping_routes

		cost = 20
		days_remove = 60
		days_re_enable = GEA_pirate_cooldown_60?60

		available = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = GEA_pirate_raid_capacity_available_tt
				check_variable = { GEA_active_raids < GEA_max_concurrent_raids }
			}
		}
		complete_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { GEA_active_raids = 1 }
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_country_flag = GEA_seamen_quality_ready }
					set_country_flag = GEA_Raid_on_the_Pacific_shipping_routes_silent
					clr_country_flag = GEA_seamen_quality_ready
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_country_flag = GEA_loyal_seamen_ready }
					set_country_flag = GEA_Raid_on_the_Pacific_shipping_routes_inspired
					clr_country_flag = GEA_loyal_seamen_ready
				}
			}
		}
		cancel_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { GEA_active_raids = -1 }
			clamp_variable = { var = GEA_active_raids min = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = GEA_Raid_on_the_Pacific_shipping_routes_silent }
				set_country_flag = GEA_seamen_quality_ready
				clr_country_flag = GEA_Raid_on_the_Pacific_shipping_routes_silent
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = GEA_Raid_on_the_Pacific_shipping_routes_inspired }
				set_country_flag = GEA_loyal_seamen_ready
				clr_country_flag = GEA_Raid_on_the_Pacific_shipping_routes_inspired
			}
		}

		modifier = {
			experience_gain_navy = 0.01
			consumer_goods_factor = -0.05
			political_power_factor = 0.05
		}

		remove_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision remove GEA_Raid_on_the_Pacific_shipping_routes"
			hidden_effect = {
				set_temp_variable = { GEA_raid_reward_multiplier = 1 }
				set_temp_variable = { GEA_raid_extra_reward_chance = 0 }
				if = {
					limit = { has_country_flag = GEA_Raid_on_the_Pacific_shipping_routes_inspired }
					set_temp_variable = { GEA_raid_reward_multiplier = 2 }
					set_temp_variable = { GEA_raid_extra_reward_chance = 15 }
				}
			}
			GEA_add_pirate_loot = yes
			GEA_pirate_offsite_civ_reward = yes
			hidden_effect = {
				add_to_variable = { GEA_active_raids = -1 }
				clamp_variable = { var = GEA_active_raids min = 0 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_country_flag = GEA_Raid_on_the_Pacific_shipping_routes_silent }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = GEA_pirate_exposure_disabled }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = GEA_pirate_exposure_disabled } }
					custom_override_tooltip = {
						tooltip = GEA_pirate_discovery_chance_increase_tt
						add_to_variable = { GEA_pirate_discovery_chance = 2 }
						clamp_variable = { var = GEA_pirate_discovery_chance min = 0 max = 100 }
					}
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					set_temp_variable = { GEA_pirate_discovery_weight = GEA_pirate_discovery_chance }
					set_temp_variable = { GEA_pirate_safe_weight = 100 }
					subtract_from_temp_variable = { GEA_pirate_safe_weight = GEA_pirate_discovery_chance }
					meta_effect = {
						text = {
							random_list = {
								[DISCOVERY_CHANCE] = {
									GEA_pirate_discovery_consequences = yes
								}
								[SAFE_CHANCE] = {
									# 未被发现，无事发生
								}
							}
						}
						DISCOVERY_CHANCE = "[?GEA_pirate_discovery_weight|0]"
						SAFE_CHANCE = "[?GEA_pirate_safe_weight|0]"
					}
				}
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = GEA_pacific_shipping_raid_target_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				random_country = {
					limit = {
						exists = yes
						OR = {
							tag = JAP
							tag = TRM
							tag = USA
							tag = LEC
							owns_state = 613 # 南京的当前拥有者
						}
					}
					add_timed_idea = { idea = GEA_shipping_routes_raided days = 30 }
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				clr_country_flag = GEA_Raid_on_the_Pacific_shipping_routes_silent
				clr_country_flag = GEA_Raid_on_the_Pacific_shipping_routes_inspired
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 0.1
		
			# Do not use automated interactions against player-controlled targets.
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = {
					GER = { exists = yes is_ai = no }
					JAP = { exists = yes is_ai = no }
					LEC = { exists = yes is_ai = no }
					TRM = { exists = yes is_ai = no }
					USA = { exists = yes is_ai = no }
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.25
				NOT = { has_political_power > 50 }
			}
		
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				613 = { owner = { is_ai = no } } # Current owner of Nanjing may also be a raid target.
			}
		}
	}

				add_to_variable = { GEA_active_raids = 2 }
			}
		}

		cancel_effect = {
			hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = GEA_manila_heist_in_progress }
			hidden_effect = {
				add_to_variable = { GEA_active_raids = -2 }
				clamp_variable = { var = GEA_active_raids min = 0 }
			}
		}
		
		remove_effect = {
			hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = GEA_manila_heist_in_progress }
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision remove GEA_Secret_operation_Manila_treasury"
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = GEA_pirate_exposure_disabled } }
				custom_override_tooltip = {
					tooltip = GEA_secret_operation_discovery_chance_tt
					add_to_variable = { GEA_pirate_discovery_chance = 10 }
					clamp_variable = { var = GEA_pirate_discovery_chance min = 0 max = 100 }
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = { GEA_roll_secret_operation_discovery = yes }
			add_timed_idea = { idea = GEA_Manila_gold days = 365 }
			add_political_power = 150
			hidden_effect = {
				add_to_variable = { GEA_active_raids = -2 }
				clamp_variable = { var = GEA_active_raids min = 0 }
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 0.1
		
			# Do not use automated interactions against player-controlled targets.
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = {
					GER = { exists = yes is_ai = no }
					PHI = { exists = yes is_ai = no }
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.25
				NOT = { has_political_power > 50 }
			}
		}
	}
	GEA_Secret_operation_Black_gold = { #秘密行动：黑金
		allowed = { original_tag = GEA }
		icon = GFX_decision_GEA_Secret_operation_Black_gold

		custom_cost_text = GEA_niagara_heist_cost
		custom_cost_trigger = { command_power > 99.99 has_navy_experience > 49.99 }

		days_remove = 120
		fire_only_once = yes

		visible = { has_completed_focus = GEA_The_secret_operation_under_the_glow_of_black_white_and_red_lights }

		available = {
			JAP = { exists = yes }
			OR = {
				AND = { #美国尚未爆发内战，美日贸易仍然开放
					NOT = { has_global_flag = USA_civil_war_begun_flag }
					USA = { exists = yes }
					JAP = { NOT = { has_war_with = USA } }
				}
				AND = { #内战爆发后，由太平洋国继续对日贸易
					has_global_flag = USA_civil_war_begun_flag
					CAL = { exists = yes }
					JAP = { NOT = { has_war_with = CAL } }
				}
			}
			has_manpower > 1000
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = GEA_niagara_preparation_available_tt
				check_variable = { GEA_secret_operation_preparations > 0 }
			}
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = GEA_two_pirate_fleets_available_tt
				OR = {
					AND = { check_variable = { GEA_max_concurrent_raids > 1 } check_variable = { GEA_active_raids < 1 } }
					AND = { check_variable = { GEA_max_concurrent_raids > 2 } check_variable = { GEA_active_raids < 2 } }
					AND = { check_variable = { GEA_max_concurrent_raids > 3 } check_variable = { GEA_active_raids < 3 } }
					AND = { check_variable = { GEA_max_concurrent_raids > 4 } check_variable = { GEA_active_raids < 4 } }
					AND = { check_variable = { GEA_max_concurrent_raids > 5 } check_variable = { GEA_active_raids < 5 } }
					AND = { check_variable = { GEA_max_concurrent_raids > 6 } check_variable = { GEA_active_raids < 6 } }
				}
			}
		}

		complete_effect = {
			add_command_power = -100
			navy_experience = -50
			custom_override_tooltip = {
				tooltip = GEA_niagara_preparation_spent_tt
				subtract_from_variable = { GEA_secret_operation_preparations = 1 }
			}
			custom_override_tooltip = {
				tooltip = GEA_niagara_two_fleets_assigned_tt
				add_to_variable = { GEA_active_raids = 2 }
			}
		}

		cancel_effect = {
			hidden_effect = {
				add_to_variable = { GEA_active_raids = -2 }
				clamp_variable = { var = GEA_active_raids min = 0 }
			}
		}
		
		remove_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision remove GEA_Secret_operation_Black_gold"
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = GEA_pirate_exposure_disabled } }
				custom_override_tooltip = {
					tooltip = GEA_secret_operation_discovery_chance_tt
					add_to_variable = { GEA_pirate_discovery_chance = 10 }
					clamp_variable = { var = GEA_pirate_discovery_chance min = 0 max = 100 }
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = { GEA_roll_secret_operation_discovery = yes }
			custom_override_tooltip = {
				tooltip = GEA_black_gold_fill_fuel_tt
				set_fuel_ratio = 1
			}
			add_resource = { type = oil amount = 100 state = 336 days = 120 }
			set_temp_variable = { GEA_pirate_loot_change_amount = 2 }
			GEA_change_pirate_loot_effect = yes
			JAP = { add_timed_idea = { idea = GEA_oil_trade_routes_disrupted days = 120 } }
			hidden_effect = {
				add_to_variable = { GEA_active_raids = -2 }
				clamp_variable = { var = GEA_active_raids min = 0 }
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 0.1
		
			# Do not use automated interactions against player-controlled targets.
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = {
					CAL = { exists = yes is_ai = no }
					GER = { exists = yes is_ai = no }
					JAP = { exists = yes is_ai = no }
					USA = { exists = yes is_ai = no }
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.25
				NOT = { has_political_power > 50 }
			}
		}
	}
